```
1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { computed } from 'vue';
3	import type { FrameFieldDefinition } from '@/features/frame';
4	import type { SendFieldValue } from '@/features/send';
5	
6	// --- Types ---
7	
8	interface FieldSection {
9	  readonly key: string;
10	  readonly label: string;
11	  readonly color: string;
12	  readonly fields: readonly FrameFieldDefinition[];
13	}
14	
15	// --- Props & Emits ---
16	
17	const props = defineProps<{
18	  readonly fields: readonly FrameFieldDefinition[];
19	  readonly values: Record<string, SendFieldValue>;
20	  readonly direction: 'send' | 'receive';
21	}>();
22	
23	const emit = defineEmits<{
24	  'update:values': [values: Record<string, SendFieldValue>];
25	}>();
26	
27	// --- Section definitions (O4: module-level constants) ---
28	
29	const SECTION_DEFS = [
30	  { key: 'configurable', label: '可调字段', color: 'info' },
31	  { key: 'expression', label: '计算参数', color: 'warning' },
32	  { key: 'fixed', label: '不可配置', color: 'grey' },
33	] as const;
34	
35	// --- Classification ---
36	
37	function classifyField(field: FrameFieldDefinition): 'configurable' | 'expression' | 'fixed' {
38	  if (field.configurable) return 'configurable';
39	  if (field.expressionConfig) return 'expression';
40	  return 'fixed';
41	}
42	
43	const sections = computed<readonly FieldSection[]>(() => {
44	  const buckets = new Map<string, FrameFieldDefinition[]>();
45	  for (const def of SECTION_DEFS) {
46	    buckets.set(def.key, []);
47	  }
48	  for (const field of props.fields) {
49	    const bucket = classifyField(field);
50	    buckets.get(bucket)!.push(field);
51	  }
52	  return SECTION_DEFS
53	    .map((def) => ({
54	      key: def.key,
55	      label: def.label,
56	      color: def.color,
57	      fields: buckets.get(def.key)!,
58	    }))
59	    .filter((section) => section.fields.length > 0);
60	});
61	
62	// --- Helpers ---
63	
64	const NUMERIC_DATA_TYPES = new Set([
65	  'uint8', 'int8', 'uint16', 'int16', 'uint32', 'int32',
66	  'uint64', 'int64', 'float', 'double',
67	]);
68	
69	function isNumericField(field: FrameFieldDefinition): boolean {
70	  return NUMERIC_DATA_TYPES.has(field.dataType);
71	}
72	
73	function hasNonTrivialFactor(field: FrameFieldDefinition): boolean {
74	  return field.factor !== undefined && field.factor !== 1;
75	}
76	
77	function getDisplayValue(field: FrameFieldDefinition): string {
78	  const val = props.values[field.id];
79	  if (val !== undefined && val !== null) return String(val);
80	  return '--';
81	}
82	
83	function resolveFieldInputValue(field: FrameFieldDefinition): SendFieldValue {
84	  return props.values[field.id] ?? field.defaultValue ?? '';
85	}
86	
87	// --- Mutation ---
88	
89	function onFieldChange(fieldId: string, value: SendFieldValue): void {
90	  emit('update:values', { ...props.values, [fieldId]: value });
91	}
92	
93	function onNumericInput(field: FrameFieldDefinition, raw: string | number | null): void {
94	  if (raw === null || raw === '') {
95	    onFieldChange(field.id, '');
96	    return;
97	  }
98	  const num = Number(raw);
99	  onFieldChange(field.id, Number.isNaN(num) ? String(raw) : num);
100	}
101	</script>
102	
103	<template>
104	  <div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
105	    <template v-for="(section, si) in sections" :key="section.key">
106	      <q-separator v-if="si > 0" />
107	
108	      <div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
109	        <q-badge :color="section.color" :label="section.label" />
110	
111	        <div class="flex flex-col gap-3 pl-2">
112	          <template v-for="field in section.fields" :key="field.id">
113	
114	            <!-- ===== Configurable — input ===== -->
115	            <template v-if="section.key === 'configurable' && field.inputType === 'input'">
116	              <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
117	                <span class="rw-text-label text-xs" style="min-width: 80px">
118	                  {{ field.name }}
119	                  <template v-if="hasNonTrivialFactor(field)">
120	                    &nbsp;(x{{ field.factor }})
121	                  </template>
122	                </span>
123	                <q-input
124	                  outlined
125	                  dense
126	                  :type="isNumericField(field) ? 'number' : 'text'"
127	                  :model-value="resolveFieldInputValue(field)"
128	                  class="flex-1"
129	                  @update:model-value="onNumericInput(field, $event)"
130	                />
131	                <span v-if="field.description" class="rw-text-desc text-xs">
132	                  {{ field.description }}
133	                </span>
134	              </div>
135	            </template>
136	
137	            <!-- ===== Configurable — select ===== -->
138	            <template v-else-if="section.key === 'configurable' && field.inputType === 'select'">
139	              <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
140	                <span class="rw-text-label text-xs" style="min-width: 80px">
141	                  {{ field.name }}
142	                </span>
143	                <q-select
144	                  outlined
145	                  dense
146	                  emit-value
147	                  map-options
148	                  :options="field.options"
149	                  :model-value="resolveFieldInputValue(field)"
150	                  class="flex-1"
151	                  @update:model-value="onFieldChange(field.id, $event)"
152	                />
153	                <span v-if="field.description" class="rw-text-desc text-xs">
154	                  {{ field.description }}
155	                </span>
156	              </div>
157	            </template>
158	
159	            <!-- ===== Configurable — radio ===== -->
160	            <template v-else-if="section.key === 'configurable' && field.inputType === 'radio'">
161	              <div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
162	                <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">
163	                  {{ field.name }}
164	                </span>
165	                <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
166	                  <q-radio
167	                    v-for="opt in field.options"
168	                    :key="opt.value"
169	                    dense
170	                    :val="opt.value"
171	                    :label="opt.label"
172	                    :model-value="resolveFieldInputValue(field)"
173	                    color="primary"
174	                    @update:model-value="onFieldChange(field.id, $event)"
175	                  />
176	                </div>
177	              </div>
178	            </template>
179	
180	            <!-- ===== Read-only: expression or fixed ===== -->
181	            <template v-else>
182	              <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
183	                <span class="rw-text-label text-xs" style="min-width: 80px">
184	                  {{ field.name }}
185	                  <template v-if="hasNonTrivialFactor(field)">
186	                    &nbsp;(x{{ field.factor }})
187	                  </template>
188	                </span>
189	                <span class="rw-text-value text-sm">
190	                  {{ getDisplayValue(field) }}
191	                </span>
192	                <span v-if="field.description" class="rw-text-desc text-xs">
193	                  {{ field.description }}
194	                </span>
195	              </div>
196	            </template>
197	
198	          </template>
199	        </div>
200	      </div>
201	    </template>
202	  </div>
203	</template>
204	
```

```
1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { computed, ref, shallowRef, watch } from 'vue';
3	import { useQuasar } from 'quasar';
4	import { useRewriteRuntime } from '@/app/rewriteRuntime';
5	import DataTable from '@/widgets/DataTable.vue';
6	import SendTargetSelector from '@/features/send/components/SendTargetSelector.vue';
7	import { instanceColumns, type InstanceTableRow } from '@/features/send/components/instance-columns';
8	import { useSendInstances } from '@/features/send/composables/use-send-instances';
9	import { useFramePreview } from '@/features/send/composables/use-frame-preview';
10	import { useNotify } from '@/shared/composables';
11	import { listFrameAssetSummaries, cloneFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
12	import type { ReadonlyFrameAsset, FrameAssetSummary } from '@/features/frame';
13	import type { SendFieldValue, SendFrameInstance } from '@/features/send';
14	
15	const $q = useQuasar();
16	const notify = useNotify();
17	const runtime = useRewriteRuntime();
18	const frameService = runtime.features.frameService;
19	const sendService = runtime.features.sendService;
20	const connectionService = runtime.features.connectionService;
21	
22	const sendInstances = useSendInstances();
23	
24	// ===== Left column: frame list =====
25	
26	const searchText = ref('');
27	const favoriteOnly = ref(false);
28	
29	// Reactive refresh — service state is non-reactive, track mutations manually
30	const refreshKey = ref(0);
31	function refreshFrameList(): void {
32	  refreshKey.value++;
33	}
34	
35	const frameSummaries = computed<FrameAssetSummary[]>(() => {
36	  void refreshKey.value;
37	  return listFrameAssetSummaries(frameService.getSnapshot(), {
38	    direction: 'send',
39	    query: searchText.value || undefined,
40	    favoriteOnly: favoriteOnly.value || undefined,
41	  });
42	});
43	
44	const favoriteFrames = computed(() =>
45	  frameSummaries.value.filter((f) => f.isFavorite),
46	);
47	
48	const otherFrames = computed(() =>
49	  frameSummaries.value.filter((f) => !f.isFavorite),
50	);
51	
52	function getDefaultFieldValues(frame: ReadonlyFrameAsset): Record<string, SendFieldValue> {
53	  const values: Record<string, SendFieldValue> = {};
54	  for (const field of frame.fields) {
55	    if (!field.configurable) continue;
56	    if (field.defaultValue !== undefined) {
57	      const num = Number(field.defaultValue);
58	      values[field.id] = !Number.isNaN(num) && field.defaultValue.trim() !== '' ? num : field.defaultValue;
59	    } else if (field.options.length > 0) {
60	      const defaultOpt = field.options.find(o => o.isDefault);
61	      values[field.id] = defaultOpt ? Number(defaultOpt.value) || defaultOpt.value : Number(field.options[0].value) || field.options[0].value;
62	    }
63	  }
64	  return values;
65	}
66	
67	function onFrameClick(frame: FrameAssetSummary): void {
68	  const fullFrame = frameService.getFrame(frame.id);
69	  if (!fullFrame) return;
70	  const defaultValues = getDefaultFieldValues(fullFrame);
71	  const instance = sendInstances.createInstance(frame.id, frame.name, defaultValues);
72	  sendInstances.selectInstance(instance.instanceId);
73	}
74	
75	function toggleFavorite(frame: FrameAssetSummary): void {
76	  const full = frameService.getFrame(frame.id);
77	  if (!full) return;
78	  const cloned = cloneFrameAsset(full);
79	  cloned.isFavorite = !cloned.isFavorite;
80	  const result = frameService.upsertFrame(cloned);
81	  if (result.ok) {
82	    refreshFrameList();
83	  } else {
84	    $q.notify({ type: 'negative', message: '操作失败' });
85	  }
86	}
87	
88	// ===== Middle column: instance table =====
89	
90	const tableRows = shallowRef<InstanceTableRow[]>([]);
91	const selectedRows = shallowRef<InstanceTableRow[]>([]);
92	
93	watch(sendInstances.instances, (instances) => {
94	  tableRows.value = instances.map((inst, index) => ({
95	    instanceId: inst.instanceId,
96	    name: inst.name,
97	    fieldCount: Object.keys(inst.userFieldValues).length,
98	    sendCount: inst.sendCount,
99	    lastSendAt: inst.lastSendAt,
100	    description: inst.description,
101	    _index: index + 1,
102	  }));
103	}, { immediate: true });
104	
105	watch([tableRows, sendInstances.selectedInstanceId], ([rows, id]) => {
106	  selectedRows.value = id ? rows.filter((r) => r.instanceId === id) : [];
107	}, { immediate: true });
108	
109	function onRowClick(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
110	  sendInstances.selectInstance(row.instanceId);
111	}
112	
113	function onSelectionChange(selected: InstanceTableRow[]): void {
114	  if (selected.length > 0) {
115	    sendInstances.selectInstance(selected[0].instanceId);
116	  } else {
117	    sendInstances.selectInstance(null);
118	  }
119	}
120	
121	function onEditInstance(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
122	  const inst = sendInstances.instances.value.find(i => i.instanceId === row.instanceId);
123	  if (!inst) return;
124	  editingInstanceId.value = inst.instanceId;
125	  editValues.value = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(inst.userFieldValues));
126	  editDescription.value = inst.description ?? '';
127	  editName.value = inst.name;
128	  showEditDialog.value = true;
129	}
130	
131	function onCloneInstance(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
132	  const cloned = sendInstances.cloneInstance(row.instanceId);
133	  if (cloned) {
134	    notify.success('已复制');
135	  }
136	}
137	
138	function onRemoveInstance(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
139	  $q.dialog({
140	    title: '确认删除',
141	    message: `确定要删除实例「${row.name}」吗？此操作不可撤销。`,
142	    cancel: true,
143	    persistent: false,
144	  }).onOk(() => {
145	    sendInstances.removeInstance(row.instanceId);
146	  });
147	}
148	
149	function onMoveUp(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
150	  sendInstances.moveInstanceUp(row.instanceId);
151	}
152	
153	function onMoveDown(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
154	  sendInstances.moveInstanceDown(row.instanceId);
155	}
156	
157	// ===== Right column: preview + send =====
158	
159	const selectedInstance = computed<SendFrameInstance | null>(() => {
160	  const id = sendInstances.selectedInstanceId.value;
161	  if (!id) return null;
162	  return sendInstances.instances.value.find(i => i.instanceId === id) ?? null;
163	});
164	
165	const selectedFrame = computed<ReadonlyFrameAsset | null>(() => {
166	  if (!selectedInstance.value) return null;
167	  return frameService.getFrame(selectedInstance.value.frameId) ?? null;
168	});
169	
170	const selectedValues = computed<Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>>(() => {
171	  return selectedInstance.value?.userFieldValues ?? {};
172	});
173	
174	const { fullPreview } = useFramePreview(selectedFrame, selectedValues);
175	
176	const selectedTargetId = ref<string | null>(null);
177	const isSending = ref(false);
178	
179	async function onSend(): Promise<void> {
180	  const inst = selectedInstance.value;
181	  if (!inst || !selectedTargetId.value) return;
182	
183	  isSending.value = true;
184	  try {
185	    const result = await sendService.execute({
186	      frameId: inst.frameId,
187	      targetId: selectedTargetId.value,
188	      userFieldValues: inst.userFieldValues,
189	      context: { source: 'user' },
190	    });
191	
192	    if (result.kind === 'sent') {
193	      notify.success('发送成功');
194	      sendInstances.incrementSendCount(inst.instanceId);
195	    } else {
196	      notify.error('发送失败', result.error?.message);
197	      if (result.buildIssues.length > 0) {
198	        const msgs = result.buildIssues
199	          .filter(i => i.severity === 'error')
200	          .map(i => i.message)
201	          .join('; ');
202	        if (msgs) {
203	          notify.error('构建问题', msgs);
204	        }
205	      }
206	    }
207	  } catch (err) {
208	    notify.error('发送异常', err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
209	  } finally {
210	    isSending.value = false;
211	  }
212	}
213	
214	// ===== Instance edit dialog =====
215	
216	const showEditDialog = ref(false);
217	const editingInstanceId = ref<string | null>(null);
218	const editValues = ref<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>({});
219	const editDescription = ref('');
220	const editName = ref('');
221	const isSaving = ref(false);
222	
223	const isValid = computed(() => {
224	  return editName.value.trim().length > 0;
225	});
226	
227	function onEditDialogHide(): void {
228	  editingInstanceId.value = null;
229	  editValues.value = {};
230	  editDescription.value = '';
231	  editName.value = '';
232	}
233	
234	async function onEditConfirm(): Promise<void> {
235	  if (!editingInstanceId.value || !isValid.value) return;
236	
237	  isSaving.value = true;
238	  try {
239	    sendInstances.updateInstance(editingInstanceId.value, {
240	      name: editName.value,
241	      description: editDescription.value,
242	      userFieldValues: { ...editValues.value },
243	    });
244	    showEditDialog.value = false;
245	  } finally {
246	    isSaving.value = false;
247	  }
248	}
249	
250	// Configurable fields of selected frame for right panel display
251	const configurableFields = computed(() => {
252	  const frame = selectedFrame.value;
253	  if (!frame) return [];
254	  return frame.fields.filter(f => f.configurable);
255	});
256	</script>

361	          </div>
362	        </div>
363	      </div>
364	
365	      <!-- Middle column: instance table (flex:1) -->
366	      <div class="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
367	        <DataTable
368	          :columns="instanceColumns"
369	          :rows="tableRows"
370	          row-key="instanceId"
371	          selection="single"
372	          :selected="selectedRows"
373	          container-height="calc(100vh - 100px)"
374	          @row-click="(_row: InstanceTableRow) => onRowClick(_row)"
375	          @update:selected="onSelectionChange"
376	        >
377	          <template #no-data>
378	            <div class="text-center w-full q-pa-lg rw-text-label">
379	              暂无帧实例，从左侧选择帧格式创建
380	            </div>
381	          </template>
382	
383	          <template #body-cell-_index="props">
384	            <q-td :props="props">
385	              {{ props.row._index }}
386	            </q-td>
387	          </template>
388	
389	          <template #body-cell-_actions="props">
390	            <q-td :props="props">
391	              <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
392	                <q-btn flat round dense icon="o_edit" size="sm" color="primary" @click.stop="onEditInstance(props.row)" />
393	                <q-btn flat round dense icon="o_content_copy" size="sm" color="primary" @click.stop="onCloneInstance(props.row)" />
394	                <q-btn flat round dense icon="o_delete" size="sm" color="negative" @click.stop="onRemoveInstance(props.row)" />
395	                <q-btn flat round dense icon="o_arrow_upward" size="sm" color="grey" :disable="props.row._index <= 1" @click.stop="onMoveUp(props.row)" />
396	                <q-btn flat round dense icon="o_arrow_downward" size="sm" color="grey" :disable="props.row._index >= tableRows.length" @click.stop="onMoveDown(props.row)" />
397	              </div>
398	            </q-td>
399	          </template>
400	        </DataTable>
401	      </div>
402	
403	      <!-- Right column: preview + send (300px) -->
404	      <div class="w-[300px] flex-shrink-0 flex flex-col overflow-y-auto border-l rw-divider-l">
405	        <template v-if="selectedInstance">
406	          <!-- Instance info -->
407	          <div class="p-4 rw-divider-b">
408	            <div class="mb-2">
409	              <span class="rw-text-label">名称</span>
410	              <div class="rw-text-value">{{ selectedInstance.name }}</div>
411	            </div>
412	            <div class="mb-2">
413	              <span class="rw-text-label">创建时间</span>
414	              <div class="rw-text-value text-sm">{{ selectedInstance.createdAt }}</div>
415	            </div>
416	            <div v-if="selectedInstance.description">
417	              <span class="rw-text-label">备注</span>
418	              <div class="rw-text-desc text-sm">{{ selectedInstance.description }}</div>
419	            </div>
420	          </div>
421	
422	          <!-- Hex preview -->
423	          <div class="p-4 rw-divider-b">
424	            <span class="rw-text-label">帧预览 (Hex)</span>
425	            <pre v-if="fullPreview.hexPreview" class="font-mono rw-text-value text-xs mt-1 p-2 rounded" style="background: var(--rw-color-surface-elevated); white-space: pre-wrap; word-break: break-all;">{{ fullPreview.hexPreview }}</pre>
426	            <div v-else class="rw-text-desc text-xs mt-1">无预览数据</div>
427	          </div>
428	
429	          <!-- Configurable fields -->
430	          <div v-if="configurableFields.length > 0" class="p-4 rw-divider-b">
431	            <span class="rw-text-label">参数值</span>
432	            <div class="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-1">
433	              <div
434	                v-for="field in configurableFields"
435	                :key="field.id"

437	              >
438	                <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">{{ field.name }}</span>
439	                <span class="rw-text-value text-xs">{{ selectedInstance.userFieldValues[field.id] ?? '--' }}</span>
440	              </div>
441	            </div>
442	          </div>
443	
444	          <!-- Preview issues -->
445	          <div v-if="fullPreview.issues.length > 0" class="p-4 rw-divider-b">
446	            <span class="rw-text-label">构建问题</span>
447	            <div class="mt-1 flex flex-col gap-1">
448	              <div
449	                v-for="(issue, idx) in fullPreview.issues"
450	                :key="idx"
451	                class="rw-text-desc text-xs"
452	                :class="issue.severity === 'error' ? 'text-negative' : 'text-warning'"
453	              >

455	              </div>
456	            </div>

487	        </div>
```

```
Now I have all the information I need to review these files. Let me analyze each file against the conventions:

## File Review Report

### 1. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/pages/SendPage.vue`

**PASS Items:**
- **P4**: Lines 91-104 - Correctly uses `shallowRef` + `watch` for `tableRows` and `selectedRows` derived arrays
- **P6**: Lines 309, 343 - v-for uses stable unique keys (`frame.id`)
- **P8**: No inline `.filter()/.map()/.reduce()` in templates - all extracted to computed/watch
- **O4**: Lines 27-33 - `SECTION_DEFS` defined at module level in FieldEditWidget (imported component)
- **D1/D2**: Line 503 - QDialog uses `v-model="showEditDialog"` with `@hide="onEditDialogHide"`
- **D2**: Line 504 - Dialog uses `rw-dialog-lg` class
- **F4**: Lines 480-490, 541-549 - Submit buttons have `:loading` and `:disable`
- **CM3**: Line 17 - Uses `useNotify()` for notifications (lines 135, 194, 197, 204, 209)
- **T2**: Lines 1-77 - Column definitions extracted to separate `instance-columns.ts` file
- **C4**: Lines 298, 305, 318, 339, 420, 421, 424, 428, 435, 442, 449, 456, 473, 495 - Uses `rw-text-label/value/desc` classes instead of inline color tokens
- **Layout**: Lines 271-500 - Uses UnoCSS classes (gap-3, p-4, flex, etc.)

**FAIL Items:**
- **CM1**: Lines 180-213 - `onSend()` function manually manages `isSending` state with try-finally instead of using `useAsyncAction()` composable
  - **Fix**: Should use `const { execute, isOperating } = useAsyncAction();` pattern

**Minor Issues:**
- Line 85: Uses `$q.notify()` directly instead of `useNotify()` for negative notification
- Lines 45-51: Uses `computed()` with `.filter()` for `favoriteFrames` and `otherFrames` (acceptable for small lists per P4 exception)

---

### 2. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/widgets/FieldEditWidget.vue`

**PASS Items:**
- **P4**: Line 43 - Uses `computed()` for `sections` (acceptable as it's a computed transformation, not a large array)
- **P6**: Lines 105, 112 - v-for uses stable unique keys (`section.key`, `field.id`)
- **P8**: No inline `.filter()/.map()/.reduce()` in templates
- **O4**: Lines 29-33 - `SECTION_DEFS` defined at module level as constant
- **C4**: Lines 117, 131, 132, 140, 153, 154, 162, 183, 189, 192 - Uses `rw-text-label/value/desc` classes
- **Layout**: Lines 104-202 - Uses UnoCSS classes (flex, gap-3, p-2, etc.)

**FAIL Items:**
- **C4**: Lines 117, 140, 183 - Inline `style="min-width: 80px"` should be extracted to semantic class
  - **Fix**: Create utility class like `rw-label-min-width` or use existing spacing utilities

---

### 3. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/send/composables/use-send-instances.ts`

**PASS Items:**

- **P2**: Lines 38, 57-59, 84, 98-100 - Updates use array replacement pattern (`instances.value = [...instances.value, item]` or `newArr[idx] = item; instances.value = newArr`)
- **P6**: Not applicable (no v-for in composable)

**FAIL Items:**

---

### 4. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/send/composables/use-frame-preview.ts`

**PASS Items:**

### 5. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/send/components/SendTargetSelector.vue`

**PASS Items:**

## Summary

**Overall Compliance**: The files show strong adherence to the rewrite frontend conventions, with most rules properly followed.

**Critical Issues to Fix**:
1. **SendPage.vue line 180-213**: Replace manual `isSending` state management with `useAsyncAction()` composable
2. **SendPage.vue line 85**: Replace `$q.notify()` with `useNotify()` for consistency
3. **FieldEditWidget.vue lines 117, 140, 183**: Extract inline `style="min-width: 80px"` to semantic class

**Strengths**:

- Consistent use of stable unique keys in v-for (P6)
- Good separation of column definitions (T2)
- Proper use of semantic text classes (C4)
- Correct QDialog implementation (D1/D2)
- Submit buttons properly have loading/disable states (F4)
```
